- **psci-open-science-and-transparency**: Use when meeting Psychological Science's open-science requirements. Prepares compliance; it does not waive requirements. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Psychological-Review-Skills/skills/psychrev-conceptual-exhibits) · ★1.1k)
- **psychrev-contribution-framing**: Use when articulating the theoretical advance of a Psychological Review manuscript — what the field can explain after the paper that it could not before, stated against the specific prior models it improves on. - **psychrev-literature-positioning**: Use when locating a Psychological Review manuscript within its theoretical conversation — naming the theories it extends and, above all, the rival models it must beat or subsume. Guides analysis norms; it does not fabricate results. - **pubar-literature-positioning**: Use when positioning a Public Administration Review (PAR) manuscript against the literature so it reads as a field-wide contribution that also informs practice. Stakes the contribution; it does not write the lit review. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Public-Administration-Review-Skills/skills/pubar-literature-positioning) · ★1.1k)
- **pubar-rebuttal**: Use when writing the response to a Public Administration Review (PAR) revise-and-resubmit. Structures the response letter; it does not fabricate new results. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Public-Administration-Review-Skills/skills/pubar-rebuttal) · ★1.1k)
- **pubar-research-design**: Use when defending the research design of a Public Administration Review (PAR) manuscript — public-management causal designs (DiD around reforms, survey & field experiments on bureaucrats/citizens, RD, IV), case comparison and process tracing, and mixed methods. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Public-Administration-Review-Skills/skills/pubar-research-design) · ★1.1k)
- **pubar-review-process**: Use when you need to understand how Public Administration Review (PAR) evaluates a manuscript — double-blind review, desk screening for fit/scope/practice-relevance, the reject / major / minor / accept decision spectrum, the role of Evidence for Practice, and the article types. Sets expectations and shapes the paper to survive review; it does not contact editors. - **pubar-submission**: Use when running the final pre-submission preflight for Public Administration Review (PAR) — article-type selection, double-blind preparation, the ≤ 8,000-word and ≤ 150-word-abstract caps, Evidence for Practice points, APA formatting, and TOP transparency materials. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Public-Administration-Review-Skills/skills/pubar-submission) · ★1.1k)
- **pubar-tables-figures**: Use when building tables and figures for a Public Administration Review (PAR) manuscript so exhibits are self-contained, accessible, and communicate effect magnitude to scholars and practitioners alike. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Public-Administration-Review-Skills/skills/pubar-tables-figures) · ★1.1k)
- **pubar-theory-building**: Use when building the theoretical argument of a Public Administration Review (PAR) manuscript into a contribution that advances public-management theory and informs practice. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Public-Administration-Review-Skills/skills/pubar-theory-building) · ★1.1k)
- **pubar-topic-selection**: Use when deciding whether a public-administration project fits Public Administration Review (PAR) and which article type to target. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Public-Administration-Review-Skills/skills/pubar-topic-selection) · ★1.1k)
- **pubar-transparency-and-data**: Use when preparing the transparency / reproducibility materials for a Public Administration Review (PAR) manuscript. Covers quantitative and qualitative transparency and the restricted-data path. Prepares the package; it does not waive requirements. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Public-Administration-Review-Skills/skills/pubar-transparency-and-data) · ★1.1k)
- **pubar-workflow**: Use when starting or navigating any Public Administration Review (PAR) manuscript and unsure which skill applies. - **pubar-writing-style**: Use when drafting or polishing a Public Administration Review (PAR) manuscript so it reads for scholars AND practitioners, carries strong Evidence for Practice, and fits the limits (≤ 8,000 words incl. abstract/endnotes/references; abstract ≤ 150 words).
